import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { invoke } from '@tauri-apps/api/core';

type Health = { atlas_api: boolean; llama_swap: boolean; atlas_api_detail: string; llama_detail: string };
type Metrics = { cpu_percent: number; memory_used_bytes: number; memory_total_bytes: number };
type Gpu = { name: string; vendor: string; vram_total_mb: number | null; vram_free_mb: number | null; utilization_percent: number | null; source: string };
type NodeStatus = { node_id:string; enabled:boolean; registered:boolean; remote_workloads_allowed:boolean; cpu_percent:number; memory_total_mb:number; memory_available_mb:number; gpus:Gpu[]; gateway_configured:boolean; trusted_scheduler_configured:boolean; sandbox_available:boolean; local_ledger_entries:number };
type NodeConfig = { enabled:boolean; allow_remote_workloads:boolean; allow_networked_inference:boolean; max_cpu_percent:number; max_memory_mb:number; gateway_url:string; ollama_url:string; trusted_scheduler_key_hex:string; allowed_images:string[]; registered:boolean };

function NodeDashboard() {
  const [status,setStatus]=useState<NodeStatus|null>(null); const [config,setConfig]=useState<NodeConfig|null>(null);
  const [models,setModels]=useState<string[]>([]); const [model,setModel]=useState(''); const [benchmark,setBenchmark]=useState<any>(null);
  const [network,setNetwork]=useState<any>(null); const [ledger,setLedger]=useState<any>(null); const [busy,setBusy]=useState(''); const [notice,setNotice]=useState(''); const [error,setError]=useState('');
  const refresh=useCallback(async()=>{try{const [s,c,l]=await Promise.all([invoke<NodeStatus>('node_status'),invoke<NodeConfig>('node_config_view'),invoke<any>('node_contribution_ledger')]);setStatus(s);setConfig(c);setLedger(l);}catch(e){setError(String(e));}},[]);
  const loadModels=useCallback(async()=>{try{const m=await invoke<any>('node_local_models');const names=(m.models||[]).map((x:any)=>x.name as string);setModels(names);if(names.length)setModel(current=>current||names[0]);}catch(e){setError(`Local model discovery: ${String(e)}`);}},[]);
  useEffect(()=>{void refresh();void loadModels();const t=window.setInterval(()=>void refresh(),10000);return()=>window.clearInterval(t);},[refresh,loadModels]);
  const run=async(label:string,fn:()=>Promise<any>)=>{setBusy(label);setError('');setNotice('');try{const v=await fn();setNotice(typeof v==='string'?v:JSON.stringify(v));await refresh();return v;}catch(e){setError(String(e));return null;}finally{setBusy('');}};
  if(!config)return <div className="node-page"><p>Loading Atlas Node profileâ€¦</p>{error&&<pre className="node-error">{error}</pre>}</div>;
  const save=()=>run('save',async()=>{await invoke('node_configure',{gatewayUrl:config.gateway_url,ollamaUrl:config.ollama_url,trustedSchedulerKeyHex:config.trusted_scheduler_key_hex,allowedImages:config.allowed_images});await invoke('node_update_policy',{allowRemoteWorkloads:config.allow_remote_workloads,allowNetworkedInference:config.allow_networked_inference,maxCpuPercent:config.max_cpu_percent,maxMemoryMb:config.max_memory_mb});return 'Node configuration saved.';});
  const toggle=async(enabled:boolean)=>run('consent',async()=>{await invoke('node_set_enabled',{enabled});return enabled?'Atlas Node enabled locally. Remote execution remains separately disabled.':'Atlas Node disabled; remote permissions revoked.';});
  const register=()=>run('register',()=>invoke('node_register'));
  const refreshNetwork=()=>run('network',async()=>{const v=await invoke<any>('node_network_status');setNetwork(v);return v;});
  const doBenchmark=()=>run('benchmark',async()=>{const v=await invoke<any>('node_benchmark_local',{model,prompt:'Explain in one concise sentence what local AI inference means.'});setBenchmark(v);return v;});
  const imagesText=config.allowed_images.join('\n');
  return <main className="node-page">
    <div className="node-heading"><div><div className="eyebrow">ATLAS COMPUTE NETWORK / DEVICE CONTROL</div><h1>Atlas Node</h1><p>Your hardware. Your rules. Contribute compute only when you explicitly allow it.</p></div><button className="node-refresh" onClick={()=>void refresh()} disabled={!!busy}>â†» Refresh</button></div>
    <section className="node-summary">
      <div className="node-state"><span className={status?.enabled?'pulse on':'pulse'}></span><div><small>LOCAL NODE</small><strong>{status?.enabled?'ENABLED':'DISABLED'}</strong><span>{status?.registered?'Identity registered':'Not registered with a gateway'}</span></div></div>
      <div><small>NODE FINGERPRINT</small><code>{status?.node_id?.slice(0,24)||'Generatingâ€¦'}â€¦</code></div>
      <div><small>AVAILABLE MEMORY</small><strong>{status?`${(status.memory_available_mb/1024).toFixed(1)} GB`:'â€”'}</strong></div>
      <div><small>LOCAL RECEIPTS</small><strong>{status?.local_ledger_entries??0}</strong></div>
    </section>
    <div className="node-columns">
      <section className="node-panel hardware-panel"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">01 / MACHINE PROFILE</span><h2>Hardware telemetry</h2></div><span className="live-label">LOCAL</span></div>
        <div className="metric-row"><div><small>CPU LOAD</small><strong>{status?`${status.cpu_percent.toFixed(1)}%`:'â€”'}</strong></div><div><small>SYSTEM RAM</small><strong>{status?`${(status.memory_total_mb/1024).toFixed(1)} GB`:'â€”'}</strong></div><div><small>RAM FREE</small><strong>{status?`${(status.memory_available_mb/1024).toFixed(1)} GB`:'â€”'}</strong></div></div>
        <h3>GPU devices</h3>{status?.gpus.length?status.gpus.map((g,i)=><div className="gpu-card" key={i}><div className="gpu-icon">â—ˆ</div><div className="gpu-info"><strong>{g.name}</strong><small>{g.vendor} Â· {g.source}</small></div><div className="gpu-vram"><strong>{g.vram_total_mb?`${(g.vram_total_mb/1024).toFixed(1)} GB`:'â€”'}</strong><small>VRAM {g.vram_free_mb!==null?`${g.vram_free_mb} MB free`:'telemetry unavailable'}</small></div></div>):<div className="empty-state">No supported GPU telemetry detected. CPU and memory profiling remain available.</div>}
        <div className="subsection"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">02 / LOCAL MODEL RUNTIME</span><h2>Model benchmark</h2></div><span className="live-label">LOCAL API</span></div><div className="inline-controls"><select value={model} onChange={e=>setModel(e.target.value)}><option value="">Select local model</option>{models.map(m=><option key={m}>{m}</option>)}</select><button onClick={()=>void loadModels()} disabled={!!busy}>Load models</button><button onClick={doBenchmark} disabled={!model||!!busy}> {busy==='benchmark'?'TESTINGâ€¦':'Run benchmark'}</button></div>{benchmark&&<div className="benchmark-result"><div><strong>{Number(benchmark.tokens_per_second).toFixed(1)}</strong><small> TOKENS / SEC</small></div><div><strong>{benchmark.wall_time_ms} ms</strong><small> WALL TIME</small></div><div><strong>{benchmark.generated_tokens}</strong><small> GENERATED</small></div></div>}<p className="fineprint">Single local request benchmark. Results vary with model load, quantization, thermal state and prompt; not a certification.</p></div>
      </section>
      <section className="node-panel controls-panel"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">03 / OWNER CONSENT</span><h2>Contribution controls</h2></div><span className={config.enabled?'status-enabled':'status-disabled'}>{config.enabled?'ACTIVE':'OFF'}</span></div>
        <div className="consent-card"><div><strong>Join Atlas Node</strong><p>Allow this PC to be visible as an available compute node. This does not automatically allow remote workloads.</p></div><button className={config.enabled?'danger-button':'primary-button'} onClick={()=>void toggle(!config.enabled)} disabled={!!busy}>{config.enabled?'Disable node':'Enable node'}</button></div>
        <label className="switch-row"><span><strong>Remote sandbox workloads</strong><small>Run only scheduler-signed jobs in Docker with network disabled and resource limits.</small></span><input type="checkbox" checked={config.allow_remote_workloads} disabled={!config.enabled} onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,allow_remote_workloads:e.target.checked})}/></label>
        <label className="switch-row"><span><strong>Network inference requests</strong><small>Permit this node to participate in routed inference requests.</small></span><input type="checkbox" checked={config.allow_networked_inference} disabled={!config.enabled} onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,allow_networked_inference:e.target.checked})}/></label>
        <div className="limit-setting"><div><strong>CPU ceiling</strong><b>{config.max_cpu_percent}%</b></div><input type="range" min="10" max="100" step="5" value={config.max_cpu_percent} onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,max_cpu_percent:Number(e.target.value)})}/></div>
        <div className="limit-setting"><div><strong>Memory ceiling</strong><b>{(config.max_memory_mb/1024).toFixed(1)} GB</b></div><input type="range" min="512" max={Math.max(512,Math.floor((status?.memory_total_mb||8192)*0.8/512)*512)} step="512" value={config.max_memory_mb} onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,max_memory_mb:Number(e.target.value)})}/></div>
        <div className="subsection"><div className="eyebrow">04 / SECURE REGISTRATION</div><label className="field-label">Atlas gateway URL<input value={config.gateway_url} placeholder="https://api.atlas.example" onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,gateway_url:e.target.value})}/></label><label className="field-label">Local inference endpoint<input value={config.ollama_url} onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,ollama_url:e.target.value})}/></label><label className="field-label">Trusted scheduler Ed25519 public key (hex)<input value={config.trusted_scheduler_key_hex} placeholder="64 hexadecimal characters" onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,trusted_scheduler_key_hex:e.target.value})}/></label><label className="field-label">Allowed container image digests<textarea rows={2} value={imagesText} placeholder={'registry.example/model-worker@sha256:â€¦'} onChange={e=>setConfig({...config,allowed_images:e.target.value.split('\n').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean)})}/></label><div className="inline-controls"><button onClick={save} disabled={!!busy}>{busy==='save'?'SAVINGâ€¦':'Save policy'}</button><button onClick={register} disabled={!config.gateway_url||!!busy}>{busy==='register'?'REGISTERINGâ€¦':status?.registered?'Re-register':'Register node'}</button></div><p className="fineprint">Registration uses a server-issued challenge signed by this device's Ed25519 identity. Remote workload execution requires a separately trusted scheduler key and image allowlist.</p></div>
      </section>
    </div>
    <section className="node-panel network-panel"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">05 / DISTRIBUTED COMPUTE</span><h2>Network & contribution ledger</h2></div><button onClick={refreshNetwork} disabled={!!busy}>Check network</button></div><div className="network-grid"><div className="network-card"><small>GATEWAY</small><strong>{status?.gateway_configured?'CONFIGURED':'NOT CONFIGURED'}</strong><span>{network?.available?'Reachable':'No confirmed connection'}</span></div><div className="network-card"><small>SANDBOX RUNTIME</small><strong>{status?.sandbox_available?'DOCKER READY':'NOT AVAILABLE'}</strong><span>Signed workload execution only</span></div><div className="network-card"><small>ACCOUNTING</small><strong>LOCAL RECEIPTS</strong><span>Server settlement not yet connected</span></div></div>{network&&<pre className="node-json">{JSON.stringify(network,null,2)}</pre>}<div className="ledger-head"><h3>Contribution receipts</h3><span>EXECUTED â‰  VERIFIED</span></div><pre className="node-json">{JSON.stringify(ledger||{entries:[]},null,2)}</pre><p className="fineprint">Receipts are locally recorded as unverified. Only a trusted platform-signed settlement can establish billable or payable contribution. This desktop package does not claim remote scheduling, settlement or payments are live without the matching gateway services.</p></section>
    {notice&&<div className="node-notice">{notice}</div>}{error&&<pre className="node-error">{error}</pre>}
    <footer className="node-footer">ATLAS NODE Â· LOCAL-FIRST Â· OWNER-CONTROLLED Â· FAIL-CLOSED SECURITY</footer>
  </main>;
}


type OpsEvent = { id:number; at:string; level:'INFO'|'WARN'|'ERROR'; source:string; message:string };
type OpsSample = { at:string; cpu:number; ram:number };
type OpsSnapshot = { state?: { bots?: any[]; chats?: any[]; queue?: any[]; queuePaused?: boolean }; mcp_audit?: { events?: any[] }; captured_at_unix?: number };
function MiniTrend({title,values,unit}:{title:string;values:number[];unit:string}){
  const max=Math.max(100,...values); const points=values.map((v,i)=>`${values.length<2?0:i*100/(values.length-1)},${36-(Math.min(max,v)/max)*32}`).join(' ');
  return <div className="ops-trend"><div><small>{title}</small><strong>{values.length?`${values[values.length-1].toFixed(1)}${unit}`:'â€”'}</strong></div><svg viewBox="0 0 100 40" preserveAspectRatio="none" role="img" aria-label={`${title} recent trend`}><polyline points={points} fill="none" stroke="#c99bff" strokeWidth="1.8" vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke"/><line x1="0" y1="38" x2="100" y2="38" stroke="#493660" strokeWidth=".7"/></svg></div>;
}
function OperationsDashboard(){
  const [status,setStatus]=useState<NodeStatus|null>(null); const [models,setModels]=useState<string[]>([]); const [selected,setSelected]=useState(''); const [opsSnapshot,setOpsSnapshot]=useState<OpsSnapshot|null>(null);
  const [events,setEvents]=useState<OpsEvent[]>([]); const [samples,setSamples]=useState<OpsSample[]>([]);
  const [viewerUrl,setViewerUrl]=useState(()=>localStorage.getItem('atlas.ops.viewerUrl')||''); const [terminalUrl,setTerminalUrl]=useState(()=>localStorage.getItem('atlas.ops.terminalUrl')||'');
  const [snapshotUrl,setSnapshotUrl]=useState(()=>localStorage.getItem('atlas.ops.snapshotUrl')||''); const [mode,setMode]=useState<'desktop'|'terminal'|'snapshot'>('desktop');
  const [viewerConnected,setViewerConnected]=useState(false); const [refreshTick,setRefreshTick]=useState(0);
  const push=(level:OpsEvent['level'],source:string,message:string)=>setEvents(old=>[{id:Date.now()+Math.random(),at:new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),level,source,message},...old].slice(0,150));
  const refresh=useCallback(async()=>{try{const [s,m,o]=await Promise.all([invoke<NodeStatus>('node_status'),invoke<any>('node_local_models'),invoke<OpsSnapshot>('local_ops_snapshot')]);setStatus(s);setOpsSnapshot(o);const names=(m.models||[]).map((x:any)=>String(x.name));setModels(names);setSelected(v=>v||names[0]||'');setSamples(old=>[...old,{at:new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),cpu:s.cpu_percent,ram:s.memory_total_mb?100*(s.memory_total_mb-s.memory_available_mb)/s.memory_total_mb:0}].slice(-36));setRefreshTick(v=>v+1);}catch(e){push('ERROR','LOCAL NODE',String(e));}},[]);
  useEffect(()=>{void refresh();const timer=window.setInterval(()=>void refresh(),3000);return()=>window.clearInterval(timer);},[refresh]);
  const saveViewer=()=>{localStorage.setItem('atlas.ops.viewerUrl',viewerUrl.trim());localStorage.setItem('atlas.ops.terminalUrl',terminalUrl.trim());localStorage.setItem('atlas.ops.snapshotUrl',snapshotUrl.trim());setViewerConnected(false);push('INFO','SESSION','Session and screenshot endpoints saved in this desktop profile');};
  const valid=(v:string)=>/^https?:\/\//i.test(v.trim()); const activeUrl=mode==='desktop'?viewerUrl:terminalUrl;
  const selectedIndex=models.indexOf(selected); const activeSnapshot=valid(snapshotUrl)?`${snapshotUrl}${snapshotUrl.includes('?')?'&':'?'}_atlas=${refreshTick}`:'';
  return <main className="node-page ops-page">
    <div className="node-heading"><div><div className="eyebrow">ATLAS OPERATIONS / SWARM DEBUGGER</div><h1>Agent operations</h1><p>Inspect the local node, model endpoints, telemetry trends, remote desktop and event trail.</p></div><button className="node-refresh" onClick={()=>void refresh()}>â†» Refresh all</button></div>
    <section className="node-summary ops-summary"><div><small>LOCAL NODE</small><strong>{status?.enabled?'ONLINE':'LOCAL / DISABLED'}</strong><span>{status?.registered?'Gateway identity registered':'Not registered with gateway'}</span></div><div><small>MODEL ENDPOINTS</small><strong>{models.length}</strong><span>Discovered from local model runtime</span></div><div><small>CPU LOAD</small><strong>{status?`${status.cpu_percent.toFixed(1)}%`:'â€”'}</strong><span>Host telemetry Â· 5s polling</span></div><div><small>RAM AVAILABLE</small><strong>{status?`${(status.memory_available_mb/1024).toFixed(1)} GB`:'â€”'}</strong><span>Host telemetry</span></div></section>
    <section className="ops-trends"><MiniTrend title="CPU UTILISATION" values={samples.map(x=>x.cpu)} unit="%"/><MiniTrend title="RAM UTILISATION" values={samples.map(x=>x.ram)} unit="%"/><div className="ops-sample-note">{samples.length} samples Â· rolling window Â· local host only</div></section>
    <section className="node-panel ops-bot-overview"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">LIVE LOCAL AGENT REGISTRY</span><h2>Configured bots & workload activity</h2></div><span className="live-label">3s POLL</span></div>
      <div className="ops-bot-grid">{(opsSnapshot?.state?.bots||[]).map((bot:any)=>{const chats=(opsSnapshot?.state?.chats||[]).filter((c:any)=>c.botId===bot.id);const jobs=(opsSnapshot?.state?.queue||[]).filter((q:any)=>q.botId===bot.id);const latest=chats.reduce((n:number,c:any)=>Math.max(n,Array.isArray(c.messages)?c.messages.length:0),0);const state=jobs.some((q:any)=>!q.hold)?'QUEUED':jobs.length?'HELD':'IDLE';return <div className="ops-agent-row" key={bot.id}><div className="ops-agent-name"><span className={state==='IDLE'?'pulse':'pulse on'}></span><strong>{String(bot.name||'Unnamed agent')}</strong><small>{state}</small></div><div className="ops-agent-meta"><span>MODEL <b>{String(bot.model||'â€”')}</b></span><span>CHATS <b>{chats.length}</b></span><span>MESSAGES <b>{latest}</b></span><span>QUEUE <b>{jobs.length}</b></span></div><p>{String(bot.desc||'No description configured')}</p></div>})}{!(opsSnapshot?.state?.bots||[]).length&&<div className="empty-state">Waiting for local bot state from Atlas Chat.</div>}</div>
      <div className="ops-queue-line"><strong>WORK QUEUE</strong><span>{opsSnapshot?.state?.queue?.length||0} pending</span><span>{opsSnapshot?.state?.queuePaused?'PAUSED':'QUEUE ACTIVE'}</span><span>{(opsSnapshot?.state?.bots||[]).length} configured agents</span></div>
      <p className="fineprint">This inventory is sourced from Atlas Chat's persisted bot/chat/queue state. These are configured agent personas, not separate operating-system processes; per-run PID isolation and true parallel swarm workers are not yet exposed.</p>
    </section>
    <div className="ops-grid">
      <section className="node-panel ops-agents"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">01 / INSTANCE INVENTORY</span><h2>Host & model endpoints</h2></div><span className="live-label">5s POLL</span></div>
        <div className="ops-instance selected"><div className="ops-instance-top"><span className={status?.enabled?'pulse on':'pulse'}></span><strong>LOCAL HOST</strong><small>{status?.enabled?'NODE ENABLED':'NODE NOT ENABLED'}</small></div><code>{status?.node_id||'Discovering node identityâ€¦'}</code><div className="ops-instance-meta"><span>CPU {status?status.cpu_percent.toFixed(1)+'%':'â€”'}</span><span>RAM {status?(status.memory_available_mb/1024).toFixed(1)+' GB free':'â€”'}</span><span>{status?.gpus.length||0} GPU(s)</span></div></div>
        <h3>Available model endpoints</h3>{models.length?models.map(name=><button key={name} className={'ops-model '+(selected===name?'chosen':'')} onClick={()=>setSelected(name)}><span className="pulse on"></span><strong>{name}</strong><small>{selected===name?'SELECTED':'MODEL DISCOVERED'}</small></button>):<div className="empty-state">No models returned by the local runtime yet.</div>}
        {selected&&<div className="ops-selected-detail"><small>SELECTED ENDPOINT</small><strong>{selected}</strong><span>Inventory index {selectedIndex+1} / {models.length} Â· process PID and per-agent task state are not currently exposed by the local runtime API.</span></div>}
        <p className="fineprint">This build reports real host telemetry and discovered model names. It does not mislabel model names as running bot processes. The agent-instance registry and agent heartbeat/task API still need to be connected.</p>
      </section>
      <section className="node-panel ops-viewer"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">02 / REMOTE SESSION INSPECTOR</span><h2>{mode==='desktop'?'Wayland / VNC desktop':mode==='terminal'?'SSH / terminal':'Agent screenshot feed'}</h2></div><span className={viewerConnected?'status-enabled':'status-disabled'}>{viewerConnected?'FRAME LOADED':'AWAITING SESSION'}</span></div>
        <div className="ops-mode"><button className={mode==='desktop'?'chosen':''} onClick={()=>{setMode('desktop');setViewerConnected(false);}}>DESKTOP / VNC</button><button className={mode==='terminal'?'chosen':''} onClick={()=>{setMode('terminal');setViewerConnected(false);}}>SSH / WEB TERMINAL</button><button className={mode==='snapshot'?'chosen':''} onClick={()=>{setMode('snapshot');setViewerConnected(false);}}>SCREENSHOT FEED</button></div>
        <div className="ops-screen">{mode==='snapshot'?(activeSnapshot?<img className="ops-snapshot" src={activeSnapshot} alt={`Latest remote agent screenshot, refresh ${refreshTick}`} onLoad={()=>setViewerConnected(true)} onError={()=>setViewerConnected(false)}/>:<div className="ops-screen-empty"><span>â–§</span><strong>SCREENSHOT SOURCE NOT CONFIGURED</strong><p>Enter an authenticated screenshot endpoint below. The endpoint must return an image/png or image/jpeg response.</p></div>):valid(activeUrl)?<iframe title={mode==='desktop'?'Remote VNC desktop':'Remote terminal'} src={activeUrl} onLoad={()=>setViewerConnected(true)} allow="clipboard-read; clipboard-write; fullscreen"/>:<div className="ops-screen-empty"><span>â—«</span><strong>SESSION VIEWER STANDBY</strong><p>Connect a trusted noVNC / wayvnc web endpoint or ttyd / web terminal endpoint below.</p><small>No desktop feed is being simulated.</small></div>}</div>
        {mode==='desktop'?<label className="field-label">noVNC / VNC web client URL<input value={viewerUrl} onChange={e=>setViewerUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://host.example/vnc.html?autoconnect=1"/></label>:mode==='terminal'?<label className="field-label">SSH web terminal URL<input value={terminalUrl} onChange={e=>setTerminalUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://host.example/"/></label>:<label className="field-label">Agent screenshot endpoint URL<input value={snapshotUrl} onChange={e=>setSnapshotUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://agent-host/screenshot.png"/></label>}
        <div className="inline-controls"><button onClick={saveViewer}>SAVE SESSION SETTINGS</button>{mode!=='snapshot'&&<button onClick={()=>{if(valid(activeUrl))window.open(activeUrl,'_blank','noopener,noreferrer');}}>OPEN IN WINDOW</button>}<button onClick={()=>{setViewerConnected(false);setRefreshTick(v=>v+1);}}>REFRESH FRAME</button></div>
        <p className="fineprint">Desktop and terminal views embed an existing HTTPS web client. Screenshot mode re-fetches the configured image URL every 5 seconds. Atlas does not yet provision Wayland capture, VNC servers, SSH tunnels or per-agent credentials; use a VPN or authenticated reverse proxy.</p>
      </section>
      <section className="node-panel ops-feed"><div className="panel-title"><div><span className="eyebrow">03 / DIAGNOSTIC EVENT TRAIL</span><h2>Activity & connection events</h2></div><button onClick={()=>setEvents([])}>CLEAR</button></div>{[...(opsSnapshot?.mcp_audit?.events||[]).slice(0,80).map((ev:any,i:number)=>({id:'mcp-'+i,at:ev.timestamp?new Date(Number(ev.timestamp)*1000).toLocaleTimeString():'â€”',level:ev.status==='FAILED'?'ERROR':'INFO',source:ev.tool||ev.server||'MCP',message:ev.error||ev.summary||ev.status||'MCP tool event'})),...events].slice(0,150).map(ev=><div className="ops-event" key={ev.id}><time>{ev.at}</time><b className={'level-'+ev.level.toLowerCase()}>{ev.level}</b><strong>{ev.source}</strong><span>{ev.message}</span></div>)}{!events.length&&!(opsSnapshot?.mcp_audit?.events||[]).length&&<div className="empty-state">No diagnostic events yet. MCP audit and local connection failures appear here.</div>}<p className="fineprint">Live local feed combines MCP audit events and dashboard connection errors. It does not yet claim distributed trace correlation across remote worker processes.</p></section>
    </div>
  </main>;
}
export default function App() {
  const [health, setHealth] = useState<Health | null>(null); const [metrics, setMetrics] = useState<Metrics | null>(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('Starting Atlas servicesâ€¦'); const [error, setError] = useState(''); const [ready, setReady] = useState(false); const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false); const [view,setView]=useState<'chat'|'node'|'ops'>('chat');
  const refresh = useCallback(async () => { try { const [h,m] = await Promise.all([invoke<Health>('health_check'),invoke<Metrics>('system_metrics')]); setHealth(h); setMetrics(m); if(h.atlas_api){setReady(true);setError('');} } catch(e){setError(String(e));} },[]);
  const start = useCallback(async () => {setBusy(true);setError('');setMessage('Starting local inference and Atlas APIâ€¦');try{const result=await invoke<string>('start_legacy_services');setMessage(result);await refresh();setReady(true);}catch(e){setError(String(e));setMessage('Atlas could not start. See the error and project logs.');}finally{setBusy(false);}},[refresh]);
  useEffect(()=>{void start();const timer=window.setInterval(()=>void refresh(),2500);return()=>window.clearInterval(timer);},[start,refresh]);
  const memoryPct=metrics?.memory_total_bytes?metrics.memory_used_bytes/metrics.memory_total_bytes*100:0;
  if(ready)return <div className="app-frame"><div className="frame-bar"><span>âœ¦ ATLAS // LOCAL DESKTOP</span><div className="frame-status"><i/> API ONLINE Â· MCP VIA ATLAS HOST</div><nav className="desktop-nav"><button className={view==='chat'?'selected':''} onClick={()=>setView('chat')}>CHAT</button><button className={view==='node'?'selected':''} onClick={()=>setView('node')}>ATLAS NODE</button><button className={view==='ops'?'selected':''} onClick={()=>setView('ops')}>OPERATIONS</button></nav><button onClick={()=>refresh()}>REFRESH STATUS</button></div>{view==='chat'?<iframe title="Atlas Chat" src="http://127.0.0.1:8090"/>:view==='node'?<NodeDashboard/>:<OperationsDashboard/>}</div>;
  return <main className="shell"><header><div className="brand"><span className="mark">âœ¦</span><b>ATLAS</b><small>RUST DESKTOP // LOCAL-FIRST RUNTIME</small></div><button onClick={start} disabled={busy}>{busy?'STARTINGâ€¦':'RETRY START'}</button></header><section className="hero"><div><label>DESKTOP BOOTSTRAP</label><h1>Your local AI workspace is coming online.</h1><p>The Rust desktop shell supervises only the processes it starts. Your existing Atlas web interface, chats, MCP host and local models remain in the project folder.</p><p className="message">{message}</p></div><div className="ctx">LOCAL FIRST<br/><strong>RUST + REACT</strong><br/><small>PYTHON COMPATIBILITY BRIDGE</small></div></section><section className="grid"><article><label>ATLAS API Â· 8090</label><strong className={health?.atlas_api?'ok':'bad'}>{health?.atlas_api?'ONLINE':'WAITING'}</strong><small>{health?.atlas_api_detail||'Checking readinessâ€¦'}</small></article><article><label>LLAMA-SWAP Â· 8080</label><strong className={health?.llama_swap?'ok':'bad'}>{health?.llama_swap?'ONLINE':'WAITING'}</strong><small>{health?.llama_detail||'Checking readinessâ€¦'}</small></article><article><label>CPU</label><strong>{metrics?metrics.cpu_percent.toFixed(1)+'%':'â€”'}</strong><small>System-wide usage</small></article><article><label>MEMORY</label><strong>{metrics?(metrics.memory_used_bytes/1073741824).toFixed(1)+' GB':'â€”'}</strong><small>{metrics?`${memoryPct.toFixed(1)}% of ${(metrics.memory_total_bytes/1073741824).toFixed(1)} GB`:'Waiting'}</small><div className="bar"><i style={{width:`${memoryPct}%`}}/></div></article></section>{error&&<pre className="error">{error}{'\n\n'}Check that Python 3, llama-swap and the configured files in settings.json are available. Logs: logs/atlas-server.log and logs/llama-swap.log.</pre>}<section className="activity"><label>RUNTIME CONTRACT</label><p>Existing UI and Python orchestration are retained as a compatibility layer for this test build. MCP discovery and tool execution are wired into normal model chat. Rust owns desktop startup, health checks, system telemetry and shutdown. Tool execution is audited; execution alone is never labelled VERIFIED.</p><div className="states"><span className="verified">VERIFIED Â· independent proof</span><span className="unverified">EXECUTED_UNVERIFIED Â· tool ran</span><span className="failed">FAILED Â· execution/verification failed</span></div></section><footer>ATLAS Â· NO GLOBAL TASKKILL Â· NO DATA RESET Â· EXISTING PYTHON UI PRESERVED</footer></main>;
}

#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]
mod process_supervisor;
mod telemetry;
mod evidence;
mod atlas_node;

use serde::Serialize;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;
use tauri::{Manager, State};
use std::{path::PathBuf, sync::{Arc, atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering}}};

#[derive(Default)]
struct RuntimeState { children: Mutex<process_supervisor::OwnedChildren>, shutdown_started: AtomicBool, shutdown_complete: AtomicBool }
struct NodeState { manager: Arc<atlas_node::NodeManager> }
impl NodeState { fn new() -> Self { let root = std::env::var_os("LOCALAPPDATA").map(PathBuf::from).map(|p| p.join("Atlas").join("Node")).or_else(|| std::env::var_os("XDG_DATA_HOME").map(PathBuf::from).or_else(|| std::env::var_os("HOME").map(PathBuf::from).map(|p| p.join(".local").join("share"))).map(|p| p.join("Atlas").join("Node"))).unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from("atlas-node-data")); Self { manager: Arc::new(atlas_node::NodeManager::new(root)) } } }
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct Health { atlas_api: bool, llama_swap: bool, atlas_api_detail: String, llama_detail: String }

#[tauri::command]
async fn health_check() -> Health {
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder().timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(2)).build().unwrap();
    let atlas = client.get("http://127.0.0.1:8090/api/health").send().await;
    let swap = client.get("http://127.0.0.1:8080/v1/models").send().await;
    Health {
        atlas_api: atlas.as_ref().is_ok_and(|r| r.status().is_success()),
        llama_swap: swap.as_ref().is_ok_and(|r| r.status().is_success()),
        atlas_api_detail: atlas.map(|r| format!("HTTP {}", r.status())).unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string()),
        llama_detail: swap.map(|r| format!("HTTP {}", r.status())).unwrap_or_else(|e| e.to_string()),
    }
}
#[tauri::command]
fn system_metrics() -> telemetry::SystemMetrics { telemetry::snapshot() }

#[tauri::command]
async fn local_ops_snapshot() -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder().timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(3)).build().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let state = client.get("http://127.0.0.1:8090/api/state").send().await.map_err(|e| format!("Atlas state API unavailable: {e}"))?;
    if !state.status().is_success() { return Err(format!("Atlas state API returned HTTP {}", state.status())); }
    let app_state = state.json::<serde_json::Value>().await.map_err(|e| format!("Atlas state JSON invalid: {e}"))?;
    let audit = client.get("http://127.0.0.1:8090/api/mcp/audit").send().await;
    let mcp_events = match audit { Ok(r) if r.status().is_success() => r.json::<serde_json::Value>().await.unwrap_or_else(|_| serde_json::json!({"events":[]})), _ => serde_json::json!({"events":[]}) };
    Ok(serde_json::json!({"state":app_state,"mcp_audit":mcp_events,"captured_at_unix":std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs()}))
}
#[tauri::command]
async fn start_legacy_services(state: State<'_, RuntimeState>) -> Result<String, String> {
    if state.shutdown_started.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
        return Err("Atlas is shutting down; refusing to start new services".into());
    }
    let mut guard = state.children.lock().await;
    if state.shutdown_started.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
        return Err("Atlas is shutting down; refusing to start new services".into());
    }
    guard.start().await
}
#[tauri::command]
async fn stop_owned_services(state: State<'_, RuntimeState>) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut guard = state.children.lock().await;
    guard.stop();
    Ok("Stopped only Atlas-owned child processes.".into())
}
#[tauri::command]
fn record_tool_result(tool: String, ok: bool, summary: String) -> evidence::EvidenceRecord {
    evidence::record_execution(&tool, ok, &summary)
}


#[tauri::command]
fn node_status(state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<atlas_node::NodeStatus, String> { state.manager.status() }
#[tauri::command]
fn node_config_view(state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<atlas_node::NodeConfigView, String> { state.manager.config_view() }
#[tauri::command]
fn node_identity(state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> { state.manager.public_identity() }
#[tauri::command]
fn node_set_enabled(enabled: bool, state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<atlas_node::NodeStatus, String> { state.manager.set_enabled(enabled) }
#[tauri::command]
fn node_update_policy(allow_remote_workloads: bool, allow_networked_inference: bool, max_cpu_percent: u8, max_memory_mb: u64, state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<atlas_node::NodeStatus, String> { state.manager.update_policy(allow_remote_workloads, allow_networked_inference, max_cpu_percent, max_memory_mb) }
#[tauri::command]
fn node_configure(gateway_url: String, ollama_url: String, trusted_scheduler_key_hex: String, allowed_images: Vec<String>, state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<atlas_node::NodeStatus, String> { let mut c=state.manager.config_for_ui()?; let next_gateway=gateway_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string(); if c.gateway_url!=next_gateway { c.node_token.clear(); } c.gateway_url=next_gateway; c.ollama_url=ollama_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string(); c.trusted_scheduler_key_hex=trusted_scheduler_key_hex.trim().to_string(); c.allowed_images=allowed_images.into_iter().map(|v|v.trim().to_string()).filter(|v|!v.is_empty()).collect(); state.manager.save_config(c)?; state.manager.status() }
#[tauri::command]
async fn node_register(state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> { state.manager.register().await }
#[tauri::command]
async fn node_network_status(state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> { state.manager.network_status().await }
#[tauri::command]
async fn node_submit_inference(model: String, prompt: String, max_tokens: u32, state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> { state.manager.submit_inference(model,prompt,max_tokens).await }
#[tauri::command]
fn node_execute_workload(workload: atlas_node::WorkloadEnvelope, state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> { state.manager.execute_signed_workload(workload) }
#[tauri::command]
fn node_contribution_ledger(state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> serde_json::Value { state.manager.ledger() }
#[tauri::command]
async fn node_local_models(state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> { state.manager.local_models().await }
#[tauri::command]
async fn node_benchmark_local(model: String, prompt: String, state: State<'_, NodeState>) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> { state.manager.benchmark_local(model,prompt).await }

fn main() {
    tauri::Builder::default()
        .manage(RuntimeState::default())
        .manage(NodeState::new())
        .setup(|app| { let manager = app.state::<NodeState>().manager.clone(); tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move { manager.run_agent_loop().await; }); Ok(()) })
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![health_check, system_metrics, local_ops_snapshot, start_legacy_services, stop_owned_services, record_tool_result, node_status, node_config_view, node_identity, node_set_enabled, node_update_policy, node_configure, node_register, node_network_status, node_submit_inference, node_execute_workload, node_contribution_ledger, node_local_models, node_benchmark_local])
        .build(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("error while building Atlas")
        .run(|app, event| {
            match event {
                tauri::RunEvent::ExitRequested { api, .. } => {
                    let runtime = app.state::<RuntimeState>();
                    // Prevent the first close request until the owned children have stopped.
                    // Once shutdown_complete is set, allow the programmatic exit through.
                    if !runtime.shutdown_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
                        api.prevent_exit();
                    }
                    if !runtime.shutdown_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) && runtime.shutdown_started.compare_exchange(false, true, Ordering::AcqRel, Ordering::Acquire).is_ok() {
                        let handle = app.clone();
                        tauri::async_runtime::spawn(async move {
                            handle.state::<NodeState>().manager.shutdown();
                            let state = handle.state::<RuntimeState>();
                            let mut children = state.children.lock().await;
                            children.stop();
                            drop(children);
                            state.shutdown_complete.store(true, Ordering::Release);
                            drop(state);
                            handle.exit(0);
                        });
                    }
                }
                tauri::RunEvent::Exit => {
                    // Last-resort exact-handle cleanup if the host forces process exit.
                    app.state::<NodeState>().manager.shutdown();
                    if let Ok(mut children) = app.state::<RuntimeState>().children.try_lock() { children.stop(); }
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        });
}

use std::{
    fs::{File, OpenOptions},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    process::{Child, Command, Stdio},
    sync::{
        atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering},
        Mutex,
    },
    time::Duration,
};

use serde_json::Value;
use tokio::{
    sync::Mutex as AsyncMutex,
    time::{sleep, Instant},
};

/// Owns only the exact child processes spawned by this Atlas desktop instance.
///
/// The child registry uses a short-lived std mutex so shutdown never needs to
/// `try_lock()` a Tokio mutex that may be held across an HTTP readiness wait.
/// Startup itself is serialized separately with an async mutex.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct OwnedChildren {
    children: Mutex<Vec<Child>>,
    start_lock: AsyncMutex<()>,
    stop_requested: AtomicBool,
}

pub fn project_root() -> PathBuf {
    std::env::var_os("ATLAS_PROJECT_ROOT")
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .unwrap_or_else(|| PathBuf::from(r"F:\APP-DEV\projects\atlas-chat"))
}

fn port_ready(port: u16) -> bool {
    let address = format!("127.0.0.1:{port}");
    address
        .parse()
        .ok()
        .and_then(|addr| {
            std::net::TcpStream::connect_timeout(&addr, Duration::from_millis(250)).ok()
        })
        .is_some()
}

fn ensure_not_stopping(stop_requested: &AtomicBool) -> Result<(), String> {
    if stop_requested.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
        Err("Atlas startup was cancelled because shutdown was requested.".into())
    } else {
        Ok(())
    }
}

async fn wait_http(
    url: &str,
    seconds: u64,
    stop_requested: &AtomicBool,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(2))
        .build()
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let until = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(seconds);

    loop {
        ensure_not_stopping(stop_requested)?;
        if let Ok(response) = client.get(url).send().await {
            if response.status().is_success() {
                return ensure_not_stopping(stop_requested);
            }
        }
        if Instant::now() >= until {
            return Err(format!("Service did not become ready: {url}"));
        }
        sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    }
}

fn log_file(root: &Path, name: &str) -> Result<File, String> {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("logs")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    OpenOptions::new()
        .create(true)
        .append(true)
        .open(root.join("logs").join(name))
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())
}


// Windows: prevent console-subsystem child processes from opening a visible console.
fn hide_console_window(command: &mut Command) {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
        const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x0800_0000;
        command.creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW);
    }
}

fn spawn_python(root: &Path, script: &Path) -> Result<Child, String> {
    let stdout = log_file(root, "atlas-server.log")?;
    let stderr = stdout.try_clone().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut candidates: Vec<(&str, Vec<String>)> = vec![
        ("py", vec!["-3".into(), script.display().to_string()]),
        ("python", vec![script.display().to_string()]),
    ];
    #[cfg(not(windows))]
    {
        candidates = vec![
            ("python3", vec![script.display().to_string()]),
            ("python", vec![script.display().to_string()]),
        ];
    }

    let mut last = String::new();
    for (exe, args) in candidates {
        let mut command = Command::new(exe);
        command.args(args)
            .current_dir(root)
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(Stdio::from(stdout.try_clone().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?))
            .stderr(Stdio::from(stderr.try_clone().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?));
        hide_console_window(&mut command);
        match command.spawn() {
            Ok(child) => return Ok(child),
            Err(error) => last = format!("{exe}: {error}"),
        }
    }

    Err(format!(
        "Could not start Python server. Install Python 3 and ensure 'py' or 'python' is on PATH. {last}"
    ))
}

impl OwnedChildren {
    fn register(&self, child: Child) -> Result<(), String> {
        // Recover a poisoned mutex rather than dropping ownership of a live
        // child. The registry contains only Child handles created by Atlas.
        let mut children = self
            .children
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(std::sync::PoisonError::into_inner);
        children.push(child);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Serializes startup and rolls back every child started by this attempt if
    /// any later prerequisite, readiness check, or identity check fails.
    pub async fn start(&self) -> Result<String, String> {
        let _start_guard = self.start_lock.lock().await;
        self.stop_requested.store(false, Ordering::Release);

        let result = self.start_inner().await;
        if result.is_err() {
            // Startup failure must not leave llama-swap or the API orphaned.
            self.stop();
        }
        result
    }

    async fn start_inner(&self) -> Result<String, String> {
        let root = project_root();
        let settings_path = root.join("settings.json");
        let settings: Value = serde_json::from_slice(
            &tokio::fs::read(&settings_path)
                .await
                .map_err(|e| format!("Cannot read {}: {e}", settings_path.display()))?,
        )
        .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid settings.json: {e}"))?;

        let upstream = settings
            .get("upstream_port")
            .and_then(Value::as_u64)
            .unwrap_or(8080) as u16;
        let api = settings
            .get("server_port")
            .and_then(Value::as_u64)
            .unwrap_or(8090) as u16;

        ensure_not_stopping(&self.stop_requested)?;

        if !port_ready(upstream) {
            let exe = settings
                .get("llama_swap_exe")
                .and_then(Value::as_str)
                .ok_or("settings.json missing llama_swap_exe")?;
            let config = settings
                .get("llama_swap_config")
                .and_then(Value::as_str)
                .ok_or("settings.json missing llama_swap_config")?;
            if !Path::new(exe).is_file() {
                return Err(format!("llama-swap executable not found: {exe}"));
            }
            if !Path::new(config).is_file() {
                return Err(format!("llama-swap config not found: {config}"));
            }

            let stdout = log_file(&root, "llama-swap.log")?;
            let stderr = stdout.try_clone().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            let mut command = Command::new(exe);
            command.arg("--config")
                .arg(config)
                .arg("--listen")
                .arg(format!("127.0.0.1:{upstream}"))
                .current_dir(Path::new(exe).parent().unwrap_or(&root))
                .stdin(Stdio::null())
                .stdout(Stdio::from(stdout))
                .stderr(Stdio::from(stderr));
            hide_console_window(&mut command);
            let child = command.spawn()
                .map_err(|e| format!("Could not start llama-swap: {e}"))?;
            self.register(child)?;
            wait_http(
                &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{upstream}/v1/models"),
                60,
                &self.stop_requested,
            )
            .await?;
        }

        ensure_not_stopping(&self.stop_requested)?;

        if !port_ready(api) {
            let script = root.join("server.py");
            if !script.is_file() {
                return Err(format!("Atlas server.py missing: {}", script.display()));
            }
            self.register(spawn_python(&root, &script)?)?;
            wait_http(
                &format!("http://127.0.0.1:{api}/api/health"),
                20,
                &self.stop_requested,
            )
            .await?;
        }

        ensure_not_stopping(&self.stop_requested)?;

        let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(3))
            .build()
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let health = client
            .get(format!("http://127.0.0.1:{api}/api/health"))
            .send()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Atlas API health check failed on port {api}: {e}"))?;
        if !health.status().is_success() {
            return Err(format!(
                "Port {api} is occupied by a service that did not pass Atlas health verification"
            ));
        }
        let identity: Value = health
            .json()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| format!("Atlas health response was not valid JSON: {e}"))?;
        if identity.get("server_port").and_then(Value::as_u64) != Some(api as u64)
            || identity.get("upstream_port").and_then(Value::as_u64) != Some(upstream as u64)
        {
            return Err(format!(
                "Port {api} is occupied by an Atlas API with unexpected configuration; expected server {api} and upstream {upstream}."
            ));
        }

        ensure_not_stopping(&self.stop_requested)?;
        Ok(format!(
            "Atlas services ready. API :{api}; llama-swap :{upstream}. Only child processes started by this desktop instance are owned by its supervisor."
        ))
    }

    /// Stops only processes retained in this instance's child registry.
    /// This is synchronous and does not silently skip shutdown because another
    /// async startup task happens to hold a Tokio mutex.
    pub fn stop(&self) {
        self.stop_requested.store(true, Ordering::Release);
        // Recover the registry even if a previous panic poisoned the mutex;
        // skipping cleanup would violate the owned-process lifecycle contract.
        let mut children = self
            .children
            .lock()
            .unwrap_or_else(std::sync::PoisonError::into_inner);

        for child in children.iter_mut().rev() {
            if child.try_wait().ok().flatten().is_none() {
                let _ = child.kill();
            }
            let _ = child.wait();
        }
        children.clear();
    }
}

================================================================================

"""Atlas Chat local server.
Serves index.html, saves bots and chats to data.json, and proxies /v1/* to llama-swap
(127.0.0.1:8080) with streaming. Standard library only.
Run alone: py F:\\app-dev\\projects\\atlas-chat\\server.py   (app.py also imports it)
"""

# Defaults used when settings.json is missing, unreadable, or leaves a key out.
DEFAULTS = {
    "server_port": 8090,
    "upstream_host": "127.0.0.1",
    "upstream_port": 8080,
    "data_file": "data.json",
    "llama_swap_exe": r"F:\app-dev\tools\llama-swap\llama-swap.exe",
    "llama_swap_config": r"F:\app-dev\tools\llama-swap\config.yaml",
    "embedding_model": "fastembed:BAAI/bge-small-en-v1.5",
    "distillation_model": "mentor-dense",
}


def load_settings(path=os.path.join(HERE, "settings.json")):
    """Read settings.json over DEFAULTS. Bad JSON or wrong types fall back to the default."""
    s = dict(DEFAULTS)
    try:
        with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            user = json.load(f)
        if isinstance(user, dict):
            for k, v in user.items():
                if k in DEFAULTS and isinstance(v, type(DEFAULTS[k])):
                    s[k] = v
                elif k not in DEFAULTS:
                    s[k] = v  # extra keys (e.g. bot_docs) pass through
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        pass
    return s


SETTINGS = load_settings()
DATA = SETTINGS["data_file"]
if not os.path.isabs(DATA):
    DATA = os.path.join(HERE, DATA)
UPSTREAM_HOST, UPSTREAM_PORT = SETTINGS["upstream_host"], SETTINGS["upstream_port"]
PORT = SETTINGS["server_port"]

# MCP health is measured by real child processes and MCP JSON-RPC responses.
from mcp_monitor import MCPMonitor
